### 429_0839_R · January, Thursday 1. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0839/892,0,1023,1623/full/0/default.jpg

* · inscription · top · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Theodore Roosevelt
```
> Signature written above the printed date heading on the first diary page.

*1880-01-01 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
A beautiful day; a whole party of us
went out to Jerome Park to a lunch
- a very swell one - which Elliott gave
us. Emlen drove Alice Lee in his pair
and cutter; Elliott drove Rosy Lee, Harold
Haddon drove Conie, Smith Haddon[?] drove
Leila Schuyler[?], Annie Murray and myself,
and the rest - Bamie, Rose Saltonstall,
Dick, Harry Shaw, Bell[?] Whitney,
Ike[?] Iselin, Payson, Kingsford, Alfred,
Stratton; ^in a great sleigh^ We had great fun, dancing
waltzes, polka redowas and the
Virginia Reel. In the evening Dick
and I made a most absurd call
on Fanny Smith.
```
> Second 'Haddon' written with raised/abbreviated letters ('Hadon'). Insertion 'in a great sleigh' written above line between 'Stratton;' and 'We'.

### 429_0840_L · January, Friday 2. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0840/0,0,902,1591/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-02 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
After breakfast took Alice out to drive in
the Park. At 11 our six guests went
back to Boston; we have enjoyed their
visit exceedingly. In the evening went
to a theatre party and supper with
Bell[?], Iselin, Robinson, Russell, Stratton,
the two Haddons and Bob Sedgwick -
in fact the whist club.
```

### 429_0840_R · January, Saturday 3. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0840/902,0,1004,1591/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-03 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Spent the day calling on my
various friends, of all sexes &[?] ages.
```
> '&' uncertain; could be read 'sexes rages' literally.

### 429_0841_L · January, Sunday 4. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0841/0,0,912,1634/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-04 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Spent the afternoon at the Newsboys
Lodging House. Took night
train for Boston.
```

### 429_0841_R · January, Monday 5. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0841/912,0,1006,1634/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-05 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Began work again, as usual -
up to 3 oclock. Took Jack Tebbetts
out to drive in the cart; the
horse goes beautifully now though
sometimes a little frisky. My
cutter has come on; but there is not
snow enough yet for sleighing.
Spent the evening in the club
playing whist and reading.
```

### 429_0842_R · January, Wednesday 7. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0842/872,0,1019,1615/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-07 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove in town with Jack T. in
the evening to go to a great
reception at the Homers.
```

### 429_0843_L · January, Thursday 8. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0843/0,0,895,1631/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-08 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to the Harvard Assembly,
dancing the german with
Julia Bacon. Met two very
sweet, pretty girls from
Albany - the Miss. Rathbones.
```
> 'Miss.' written with long-s form ('Mifs.').

### 429_0843_R · January, Friday 9. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0843/895,0,1012,1631/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-09 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
It was rainy, so instead of
going out in my cart I
hired a buggy, and drove
over to the Saltonstalls
where I spent the afternoon
and evening.
```

### 429_0844_L · January, Saturday 10. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0844/0,0,884,1629/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-10 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the morning I drove in
town to the Brunswick, in
my cart, and took Miss.
Grace Rathbone out for a
long drive. I dined at the
Whitneys, and went to a
very pleasant party at the
Amory's afterwards.
```
> 'Miss.' written with long-s form ('Mifs.').

### 429_0844_R · January, Sunday 11. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0844/884,0,1008,1629/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-11 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
I was requested to resign my
Sunday School Class, as I am
a Presbyterian and would
not become an Episcopalian!
As I have taught there 3½
years I thought this rather
hard.
Breakfasted in the Club,
as usual. In the afternoon
I drove over to the Saltonstalls;
took Mamie out in the
cart; stayed to tea and
spent the evening there.
```

### 429_0845_L · January, Monday 12. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0845/0,0,935,1639/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-12 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the evening I went to
the Papanti Party; these
parties are the pleasantest
of all. I am very fond
of dancing; it is my
favourite amusement,
excepting horseback riding.
```

### 429_0845_R · January, Tuesday 13. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0845/935,0,1012,1639/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-13 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Tried my horse in the sleigh
for the first time; he went
beautifully. In the evening
went to the First[?] Assembly
at Horticultural Hall - much
the largest ball I have seen
in Boston.
```

### 429_0846_L · January, Wednesday 14. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0846/0,0,919,1614/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-14 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Minot W. & I dined in town
with Harry S., afterwards
going to a very pretty
little party at Mrs.
Parkman Shaws.
```
> 'W.' could be read 'U.'; ampersand-like '&'.

### 429_0846_R · January, Thursday 15. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0846/919,0,1000,1614/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-15 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
I drove Jack Tebbets over in my
cutter to a very pleasant little
dinner at the Saltonstalls; I
sat between Mrs. Saltonstall and
Kitty Lane.
```

### 429_0847_R · January, Saturday 17. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0847/918,0,972,1602/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-17 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to a very pleasant little
lunch party given by Van
Rennsaeler[?] to the Miss.
Rathbones. In the afternoon
went to a reception at the
Minots; Grace Minot is a
marvellously pretty girl.
I am exercising pretty
regularly in the gymnasium
— single stick, vaulting,
sparring, and running
3 or 4 miles a day.
```
> 'Miss.' written with long-s form (ſs). Van Rennsaeler spelling as it appears.

### 429_0848_L · January, Sunday 18. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0848/0,0,926,1627/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-18 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Spent the evening very pleasantly
at the Rotches.
```

### 429_0848_R · January, Monday 19. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0848/926,0,965,1627/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-19 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to a very pleasant party
at the Dexters.
In the afternoon I had
taken Minot W. a long
drive in the cart. Now
that old Dick is recovering
the use of his ankle I don't
feel that he is the only
one I ought to take driving.
```
> 'Minot W.' initial could be read 'U.'

### 429_0849_L · January, Tuesday 20. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0849/0,0,937,1614/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-20 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
It was Rosy's birthday,
so I drove over to
Chestnut Hill to bring her
her ^my^ present. I spent the
afternoon at the Lees, and
dined there.
```
> Line 4: 'her' at line start with 'my' written above; unclear whether 'her' is struck through.

### 429_0849_R · January, Wednesday 21. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0849/937,0,976,1614/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-21 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Went to a rather stupid
dinner party at the
Weales[?].
```
> Faint ink; family name uncertain (Weales/Wales).

### 429_0850_L · January, Thursday 22. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0850/0,0,942,1613/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-22 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Went in town to a great
A.D.Φ dinner; over a hundred
men were present. ~~Finished[?]~~ I
left about 11, and went to
a party at the Sears, which
ended about two oclock.
```
> 'in town' may be written as one word. Struck-through word on line 3 read tentatively. 'Φ' is the Greek letter phi.

### 429_0850_R · January, Friday 23. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0850/942,0,998,1613/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-23 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Went down to Salem, with Mrs.
Saltonstall, Mrs. Lee, Rose and
Alice. I am staying in a
very curious old House, with
Ht[?] belonging to Miss. Saltonstall.
In the evening went to a
^very jolly^ ~~great~~ party at Hamilton Hall
```
> First word of line 5 unclear ('Ht'?). 'Miss.' written with long-s form. Line 7: 'great' struck and 'very jolly' written above it.

### 429_0851_L · January, Saturday 24. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0851/0,0,944,1625/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-24 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Came back from Salem;
dined at Theodore Lymans;
afterwards had a little spree
in the Porc.
```

### 429_0851_R · January, Sunday 25. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0851/944,0,984,1625/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-25 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
At last everything is settled; but it
seems impossible to realize it. I
am so happy that I dare not
trust in my own happiness. I
drove over to the Lees determined to
make an end of things at last;
it was over ^nearly^ eight months since I had
first proposed to her, and I had been
nearly crazy during the past year; and
after much pleading my own sweet,
pretty darling consented to be my wife.
Oh, how bewitchingly pretty she looked!
If loving her with my whole heart and
soul can make her happy, she shall
be happy; a year ago last Thanksgiving
I made a vow that win her I would
if it were possible; and now that I have
done so, the aim of my whole life shall
be to make her happy, and to shield
her and guard her from every trial;
and, oh, how I shall cherish my sweet
queen! How she, so pure and sweet and
beautiful can think of marrying me I can
not understand; but I praise and thank God
it is so.
```
> Line 7: 'nearly' written above 'over'; 'over' may be lightly struck through. Minor punctuation (commas around 'oh', semicolon after 'understand') read from faint marks.

### 429_0852_L · January, Monday 26. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0852/0,0,934,1589/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-26 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
First examination of the Semi-Annuals.
Went to the Papanti Hall party in
the evening; my little darling
looked so pretty; by far the prettiest
girl in the room, and the greatest
belle; she gave me her favour,
```
> Hyphen in 'Semi-Annuals' may be a colon-like mark.

### 429_0852_R · January, Tuesday 27. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0852/934,0,985,1589/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-27 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
I am only just beginning to
realize it; I still feel as if it
would turn out, as it so often
has before and that Alice will
repent.
```

### 429_0853_L · January, Wednesday 28. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0853/0,0,966,1626/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-28 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to a small party at the
Parkman Shaws; but I haven't
really cared for a party this winter.
```
> 'haven't' faint; could be 'havn't'.

### 429_0853_R · January, Thursday 29. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0853/966,0,995,1626/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-29 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Lunched at the Saltonstalls; spent the
afternoon with my sweet love;
dined at the Union Club with
Frank Lee; then went to the
Horticultural Assembly; where sweet
Alice looked if possible even
prettier than usual.
```

### 429_0854_L · January, Friday 30. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0854/0,0,933,1618/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-30 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
When I look back at the last
four months and realize the
tortures I have been through it
seems like a dream; I have
hardly had one good nights
rest; and night after night
I have not even gone to bed.
I have been pretty nearly crazy,
over my wayward, wilful
darling. But I do not think
any outsider suspected it; I
have not written a word about
it in my diary since a
year ago last Thanksgiving.
It was a real case of love
at first sight —and my first
love too.
```
> 'had' on line 5 written heavily, possibly over another word.

### 429_0854_R · January, Saturday 31. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0854/933,0,999,1618/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-01-31 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went on to tell the Family;
who of course were all
perfectly delighted and too
sweet for anything, though
very much surprised.
```

### 429_0855_L · February, Sunday 1. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0855/0,0,943,1632/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-01 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Told the Uncles and Aunts;
all of whom were very much
pleased. I still feel as if I
were in a dream; it is too
good to be true.
```

### 429_0855_R · February, Monday 2. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0855/943,0,986,1632/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-02 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Chose a diamond ring for
my darling; came on to
Boston in the afternoon.
```

### 429_0856_L · February, Tuesday 3. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0856/0,0,958,1591/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-03 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Snowing heavily; but I drove over
in my sleigh to Chestnut Hill,
the horse plunging to his belly in the
great drifts, and the wind cutting
my face like a knife. My sweet
life was just as loveable and pretty
as ever; it seems hardly possible that
I can kiss her and hold her in
my arms; she is so pure and so
innocent, and so very, very pretty.
I have never done anything to
deserve such good fortune.
Coming home I was upset in
a great drift, and dragged
about 300 yards, holding on ~~th~~ to
the reins, before I could stop the
horse. In evening went to a
party at the Rotches.
```
> Small mark after 'my' on line 2. Line 15: a short word struck before 'to'.

### 429_0856_R · February, Wednesday 4. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0856/958,0,994,1591/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-04 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Superb sleighing; took Dick out
for a long drive. The engagement
is not to come out till
a week from Monday; it is
awfully hard to keep away
from her.
```

### 429_0857_L · February, Thursday 5. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0857/0,0,923,1628/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-05 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to the Harvard Assembly
dancing with Lulu Lowell.
```
> 'dancing' written over a correction.

### 429_0857_R · February, Friday 6. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0857/923,0,1009,1628/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-06 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove over to the Saltonstalls
in my sleigh. Took dinner
there, and spent the evening
with my pretty darling.
I am almost too happy.
```
> 'too' is underlined.

### 429_0858_L · February, Saturday 7. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0858/0,0,944,1590/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-07 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove Minot Weld over to his
house and back. Great Porc
supper in evening.
```

### 429_0858_R · February, Sunday 8. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0858/944,0,994,1590/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-08 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Started at work at a Mission
School over in East Cambridge.
I have certainly got a
marvellously hightoned and honorable
set of friends; they are not brilliant,
but they are plucky, honourable and
rigidly virtuous, and with plenty of
common sense. First comes Elliott;
then Jack Elliott, West, Emlen, Alfred
and Dick Saltonstall; then
Minot Weld, John Tebbets, Harry
Chapin and Harry Shaw. And now
that I have won the sweetest
and purest of women my
happiness is indeed complete.
Took tea and spent
the evening most pleasantly
at the Whitneys.
```
> Line 4 appears 'honorable', line 6 'honourable' — both as read. First 'Elliott' on line 8 slightly uncertain.

### 429_0859_L · February, Monday 9. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0859/0,0,933,1605/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-09 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Dined at Jim Parker's; then
went to the Papanti party
which I danced with Nanna
Rotch; afterwards drove out with
Dick in his cutter to Chestnut
Hill. The thermometer was below
Zero, but we were too heavily
wrapped up to heed the cold; and
it was a splendid sleigh ride.
```
> 'which' could be 'where'; 'Nanna' as read.

### 429_0859_R · February, Tuesday 10. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0859/933,0,999,1605/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-10 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge in morning.
Went to party at Winthrops in
evening.
Thank heaven I am absolutely
pure. I can tell Alice everything
I have ever done.
```

### 429_0860_L · February, Wednesday 11. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0860/0,0,914,1615/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-11 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to a small leap year
party at the Lowells; seven of us
drove there and back in a
sleigh. We had great fun:
of course my little darling
was there, as sweet and
pretty as ever.
```

### 429_0860_R · February, Thursday 12. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0860/914,0,975,1615/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-12 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
A.D.Φ dinner of 25 men in
Town; I presided. It was one
of the pleasantest dinners I
have ever been to; the songs,
stories and speeches were all
excellent.
```
> 'Φ' is the Greek letter phi.

### 429_0861_L · February, Friday 13. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0861/0,0,941,1636/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-13 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Raining hard; I drove over in a
buggy to the Lees to dinner and,
thanks to the storm, spent the
night there. In the evening I
was all the time with my
darling Little sunshine; she is
so marvelously sweet, and
pure and loveable and pretty
that I seem to love her more
and more every time I see her,
though I love her so much now
that I really can not love her more.
I do not think ever a man loved a
woman more than I love her; for a
year and a quarter now I have
never (even when hunting) gone to sleep or
waked up without thinking of her;
and I doubt if an hour has passed
that I have not thought of her. And
now I can scarcely realize that
I can hold her in my arms and
kiss her and caress her and love
her as much as I choose.
```
> 'can not' (line 12) and 'never' (line 16) are underlined. 'Little' appears capitalized.

### 429_0861_R · February, Saturday 14. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0861/941,0,990,1636/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-14 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge in
the morning, announced my
engagement to the Club, and
"set up" champagne. In the
evening went to a leap year
party at the Fishes[?].
```
> Family name at end uncertain (Fishes/Fiskes).

### 429_0862_L · February, Sunday 15. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0862/0,0,933,1629/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-15 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
After Sunday School drove over in
my cart to the Lees. I took Alice
out for quite a long drive, al-
though it began snowing; how
pretty the little witch looked!
I spent the evening with my
darling little sunshine, and
stayed all night.
```

### 429_0862_R · February, Monday 16. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0862/933,0,1020,1629/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-16 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge in the
morning; in the evening went
in town to a boxing benefit.
```
> Faint ink.

### 429_0863_L · February, Tuesday 17. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0863/0,0,941,1611/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-17 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the evening went in town to
meet the family, who came on, to
see Alice. Took Nell out to pass
the night with me.
```

### 429_0863_R · February, Wednesday 18. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0863/941,0,968,1611/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-18 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove Nell over to the Saltonstalls
to lunch; all my family came
out; my own sweet love
looked oh so pretty; I spent
the ^afternoon^ ~~evening~~ with her; we
dined at the Lees, driving
home at 10, and spending
several hours in the Porc.
Alice has received 42 bouquets.
```
> Line 5: 'evening' struck, 'afternoon' written above. '42' is underlined.

### 429_0864_L · February, Thursday 19. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0864/0,0,925,1615/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-19 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Drove Nell in[?] to Boston in
the morning. In the evening
dined with the family at
the Brunswick, and went to
a theatre party given to Bamie
and Conie by Frank and Tom
Lee. I sat by pretty Miss Sunshine,
who "matronized"!
```
> Line 1 'in' read tentatively (could be 'out'). Small caret-like mark after 'by' on line 7, possibly an insertion point with nothing legible above. 'Miss' written with long-s form. 'Conie' as written.

### 429_0864_R · February, Friday 20. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0864/925,0,985,1615/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-20 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Spent the day with the family;
^drove^ ~~went~~ to a great family dinner
at the Lees. The ^drive^ ~~ride~~ home, in
the bright, cold moonlight over
the smooth, frozen roads was
just perfect. Then I went up to
the Pudding, where we had a
great fight; staying up till
quarter of six, when as it was
too late to go to bed I played
billiards in the club till
breakfast time.
```
> Two corrections: 'went' struck with 'drove' above; 'ride' struck with 'drive' above.

### 429_0865_L · February, Saturday 21. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0865/0,0,953,1617/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-21 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Jack Tebbets and I went in
town to see the family off;
went calling with my sweet,
pretty love; dined with "Uncle"
Bill Lee; came out to Cam-
bridge for the great annual
Porc. dinner. Got to bed at
12 oclock — after being 44 hours
without sleep.
```

### 429_0865_R · February, Sunday 22. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0865/953,0,981,1617/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-22 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
After Mission School I drove
over to the Lees; I took
my little Sunshine out
for a drive, to Forrest
Hill, and towards Milton; I had
a lovely drive, for it was a
beautiful day and I had
such a sweet, pretty companion!
```

### 429_0866_L · February, Monday 23. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0866/0,0,940,1625/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-23 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Rained or snowed all day, so
I was most happy, spending
almost all the time with my
sweet queen. Oh, how I love my
little darling! She is too sweet
to me for anything; when
we are alone I can hardly
stay a moment without
holding her in my arms or
kissing her; she is such a
laughing, pretty little witch; and
yet with it all she is so
true and so tender. I do'nt
think there was ever any one
like her.
```

### 429_0866_R · February, Tuesday 24. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0866/940,0,991,1625/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-24 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge in
the morning. In the evening
went to a surprise party at
the Rogers, given to Fanny
Peabody.
```

### 429_0867_L · February, Wednesday 25. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0867/0,0,926,1636/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-25 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
For a wonder did not go over
to Chestnut Hill; but spent the
afternoon driving in my
dogcart and the evening
in the club.
```

### 429_0867_R · February, Thursday 26. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0867/926,0,984,1636/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-26 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove over to lunch at
Chestnut Hill; took my pretty
love out in the cart in the
afternoon; in the evening drove
in with her and ^Rose^ ~~[illegible]~~ to a
dinner at the Bartletts, Sat
between Miss Whitney & Miss
~~Perkins~~ Sears, and spent the
rest of the evening with
Miss Perkins
```
> struck word under 'Rose' unreadable

### 429_0868_L · February, Friday 27. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0868/0,0,914,1601/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-27 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge in the
morning. In the afternoon
went down to Salem with
sweet Alice to pass the night
at the Lees. Salem is a quaint
old place, and I am enjoying
the visit very much; as I
could not help doing, when I
am with my darling.
```

### 429_0868_R · February, Saturday 28. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0868/914,0,999,1601/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-28 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
We Came up to ^Boston^ ~~town~~ in the
morning; had a cosy tete-a tete
lunch at Parkers. In the
evening went to a dinner
at the Somerset Club, given
by Col. Russel.
```
> 'town' under inserted 'Boston' appears struck through; uncertain

### 429_0869_L · February, Sunday 29. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0869/0,0,925,1602/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-02-29 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
After Sunday School I
as usual drove over to
Chestnut Hill, and took
my sweet darling out
in the cart for a long
drive through Forrest
Hills and Milton.
Spent the night at the
Lees.
```

### 429_0869_R · March, Monday 1. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0869/925,0,990,1602/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-01 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
At about eleven o'clock
I drove my pretty love
into Boston in the
cart to pay calls. We
lunched at Ferras[?], and
drove out in the after-
noon in time to get
ready to go to a dinner
~~at~~ given to us by
the Minots.
I enjoy these long
drives with my
darling Sunshine so
much. It is beautiful
weather; and the turnout
is really very stylish;
and my love looks
so bewitchingly pretty
perched up in the high
cart, while plucky
Lightfoot bowls us
along famously; and
I feel so proud of my
pretty companion.
```
> restaurant name 'Ferras' uncertain

### 429_0870_L · March, Tuesday 2. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0870/0,0,912,1612/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-02 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge.
Delivered a lecture before
the Nat Hist Society.
```

### 429_0870_R · March, Wednesday 3. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0870/912,0,987,1612/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-03 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to the first of a
series of very pleasant
bowling parties.
```

Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0871/0,0,936,1592/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-04 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove DeKoven[?] in my
cart to a very pleasant
dinner at the Lees. Sat
between Mrs Jack Peabody
and Fanny Peabody.
```
> name 'DeKoven' uncertain

### 429_0871_R · March, Friday 5. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0871/936,0,978,1592/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-05 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove over in the afternoon
to the Lees; took my
pretty, laughing love out
for a drive; spent the
evening and night there.
```

### 429_0872_L · March, Saturday 6. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0872/0,0,945,1592/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-06 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge
in the morning.
Dined at the Whitneys.
```

### 429_0872_R · March, Sunday 7. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0872/945,0,970,1592/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-07 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
As usual went over in my
cart to the Lees, after Sun-
day school; but my poor
little darling had a head
ache, and so we could not
go driving.
```

### 429_0873_L · March, Monday 8. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0873/0,0,935,1610/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-08 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge in
the morning.
```

### 429_0873_R · March, Tuesday 9. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0873/935,0,986,1610/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-09 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went in to a very pleasant
dinner given to Alice by Fanny
Peabody.
```

### 429_0874_L · March, Wednesday 10. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0874/0,0,927,1577/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-10 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Went to a bowling party in the
afternoon, and a stag dinner in
the evening.
These bowling parties are great
fun. There are to be six in all,
given by Alice, Rose, Rosy[?],
Frank & Tom Lee, and myself.
Each time six outsiders are
invited — Hora[?], Grant, Katy
Lowell, Harriett Lawrence, Gus
Hemminway, Bob Grant, Bob Russell.
The stag dinner was
very pleasant; Dick[?], Ralph
Ellis & Peters were awfully
funny.
```
> names 'Rosy', 'Hora', 'Dick' uncertain; small quote-like marks before 'Peters' and 'The' may be ink specks

### 429_0874_R · March, Thursday 11. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0874/927,0,961,1577/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-11 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Right after breakfast I
drove over to Chestnut Hill
in the cart. It soon began
snowing, and I spent
all day with my
sweet, pretty, pure queen,
my laughing little
love. Oh, how bewitchingly
pretty she is! I can not
help petting and caressing
her all the time; and she
is such a perfect little
sunshine. I do not believe
any man ever loved
woman more than I love
her.
```

### 429_0875_L · March, Friday 12. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0875/0,0,922,1581/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-12 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
A good deal of snow on
the ground; I made a
dismal failure of an
effort to go sleighing in
the Lee's cutter with Rose.
Big dinner in the evening.
```

### 429_0875_R · March, Saturday 13. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0875/922,0,962,1581/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-13 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
~~D~~ Went over to Cambridge
to ~~th~~ see the sparring and
wrestling. Came back in
afternoon, and drove in
town with my beloved
darling to a dinner at
the Amory's.
```
> false starts struck at line beginnings

### 429_0876_L · March, Sunday 14. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0876/0,0,906,1572/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-14 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove to Cambridge to
Sunday School, and back.
```

### 429_0876_R · March, Monday 15. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0876/906,0,970,1572/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-15 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Mrs Lee took Alice and I ^to^ over
New Bedford to see her parents. Alice
and I returned alone. I enjoyed
the day very much, with my
sweet, pretty love, in the quaint
old house, and its still queerer
inmates.
```
> 'to' written above 'over'; 'over' not struck

### 429_0877_L · March, Tuesday 16. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0877/0,0,926,1603/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-16 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
R[illegible], so I did not go back to
Cambridge, but spent the day
with my sweet Sunshine,
my own beloved little Queen.
She is too bewitching, and
too perfectly lovely with me
for anything.
```
> first word blotted/overwritten after 'R'

### 429_0877_R · March, Wednesday 17. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0877/926,0,965,1603/full/0/default.jpg

```
Drove back to Cambridge. ~~Bow~~
Bowling party in afternoon
```

### 429_0878_L · March, Thursday 18. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0878/0,0,926,1601/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-18 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Dear old West[?] came on to day,
I took him over in the cart to
dine at Chestnut Hill, and
spent the late evening in the
Porc.
The dear old man has been
rejected by Fanny. I think she
has behaved abominably to him;
he means to try again.
```
> name 'West' uncertain (W letterform)

### 429_0878_R · March, Friday 19. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0878/926,0,986,1601/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-19 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
West[?] went back to New York. I
went out to Chestnut Hill
and drove with Alice, Rosy &
Rose to a dinner at the Minots.
```
> name 'West' uncertain

### 429_0879_L · March, Saturday 20. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0879/0,0,966,1610/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-20 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Came back to Cambridge for the
Athletic sports. In the evening
went in to a very pleasant
dinner party at the Shaws.
```

### 429_0879_R · March, Sunday 21. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0879/966,0,1004,1610/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-21 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Alice is staying at the Cabots in
town, so after Sunday school I
went in there to take tea.
```

### 429_0880_L · March, Monday 22. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0880/0,0,941,1590/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-22 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove over with Dick to a very
pleasant dinner at the Saltonstalls;
spent the night there.
```

### 429_0880_R · March, Tuesday 23. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0880/941,0,982,1590/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-23 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back with Dick to Cam-
bridge in the morning. In the
afternoon walked over to the Lees
to spend the night. Went to a
dinner at the Lowells in the
evening.
```

### 429_0881_L · March, Wednesday 24. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0881/0,0,931,1595/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-24 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Walked back to Cambridge in the
morning. Δ K E theatricals in the
evening.
```
> Greek letters Δ K E (Delta Kappa Epsilon)

### 429_0881_R · March, Thursday 25. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0881/931,0,977,1595/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-25 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
By way of a change spent the
day in Cambridge, in the Library,
writing up my thesis in political
economy. Since I have been
engaged I have studied very
little, and cut most of my
recitations; and I intend so to
continue during the balance
of my college course. I have
always studied well in
college, so I can afford to
cut now; and it is my last
holiday, as I shall study
law next year, and must
then do my best, and work
hard for my own little wife.
```

### 429_0882_L · March, Friday 26. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0882/0,0,955,1574/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-26 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the afternoon walked over to
Chestnut Hill to spend the night:
I passed the evening with my
own sweet little queen. It is
perfectly impossible to tell how
much I love her; it is not merely
thinking of her all the time,
it is much more than that;
she is always present in my
mind. She is so sweet and
tender and loving, and so
absolutely pure and innocent,
and with such a laughing,
sunshiny temper; and, oh, so
bewitchingly pretty. I can
never thank Heaven enough that
I am perfectly pure; and can
tell her everything I have ever done;
and so can she tell me; there
is not one thing secret between us.
"When first we love we seldom wed";
but we shall prove exceptions
to this rule.
```
> 'always' and 'everything' underlined in original; 'with' appears written over a false start

### 429_0882_R · March, Saturday 27. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0882/955,0,983,1574/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-27 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went back to Cambridge to
see the sparring and wrestling.
```

### 429_0883_L · March, Sunday 28. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0883/0,0,945,1597/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-28 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Snowing; so after Sunday
school I drove over to Chestnut
Hill in a buggy, to pass the
night.
```

### 429_0883_R · March, Monday 29. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0883/945,0,974,1597/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-29 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge; and
in evening returned to Chestnut
Hill to pass the night.
```

### 429_0884_L · March, Tuesday 30. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0884/0,0,957,1605/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-30 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge. Went
to a party at the Bryants.
```

### 429_0884_R · March, Wednesday 31. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0884/957,0,1010,1605/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-03-31 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Bowling party in afternoon.
then went to a dinner at
Youngs, for "Big six":—
Harry, ^Shaw,^ Harry Chapin, Minot
Weld, Jack Tebbets[?], Dick
Saltonstall and myself.
Went to a very funny
piece at the theatre.
```
> 'Shaw,' inserted above line after 'Harry,'; surname 'Tebbets' uncertain

### 429_0885_L · April, Thursday 1. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0885/0,0,948,1623/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-01 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Started early in the
morning with some other
students in Adv. Zoölogy
on a collecting tour to
Nahant.
In afternoon drove over
in my cart to Chestnut
Hill; sweet little Birdie[?]
was at the Francis's, so
I called for her and
took her driving. In the
evening drove with my
darling into Boston, to the
Harvard Assembly. In
spite of being engaged my
own sweet, pretty Queen was
certainly the belle
```
> pet name 'Birdie' uncertain

### 429_0885_R · April, Friday 2. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0885/948,0,996,1623/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-02 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Came back to Cambridge for
my recitations; returned to
Chestnut Hill. Took darling
out driving. Spent the
evening with her.
I am so absolutely
happy, that I sometimes
almost feel as if it
must be a dream. No mortal
ever could be happier
than I am.
```
> 'could' underlined in original

### 429_0886_L · April, Saturday 3. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0886/0,0,939,1583/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-03 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went back to Cambridge; in
~~evenin~~ afternoon took my
sweet Queen a long drive;
how bewitchingly pretty she
is! And so sweet, and
loving and tender; and
such a laughing little
witch.
In evening we drove
in to a dinner given us
by the Rotches.
```

### 429_0886_R · April, Sunday 4. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0886/939,0,998,1583/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-04 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove to Cambridge to Sun-
-day-School - for the last
time this year - and re-
turned to Chestnut Hill.
Spent the evening, as usual,
with my sweet Queen.
```

### 429_0887_L · April, Monday 5. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0887/0,0,930,1581/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-05 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Drove my pretty love in
town. I love to have sweet
Birdie perched up beside me
in the cart; she is so sweet
and pretty. Made several
calls; one on Fanny Peabody,
whom I like more than
any ~~Bo~~Boston girls, except
Rose and Rosey.
Papanti Party in evening;
I danced with Rose S.
```
> 'Boston' appears written over a false start; 'in' at end of line 1 partly obscured

### 429_0887_R · April, Tuesday 6. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0887/930,0,984,1581/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-06 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Drove over to Dick S's; then
drove him to the Minots;
dined at Jack Tebbetts[?]
and drove him out to
Cambridge afterwords[?].
```
> surname 'Tebbetts' and spelling of last word ('afterwords'/'afterwards') uncertain

### 429_0888_L · April, Wednesday 7. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0888/0,0,944,1586/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-07 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Mrs. Lee; Alice and I
went on to New York
to stay ten days. We
were met with open
arms; Mother, Bamie, Conie[?]
and Elliott are just too
sweet to me for anything.
```
> 'Conie' reading slightly uncertain

### 429_0888_R · April, Thursday 8. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0888/944,0,986,1586/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-08 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
I have to spend most of
the days working on my
thesis.
Family party at night,
to introduce Alice - who of
course looked as pretty as a
picture.
```

### 429_0889_L · April, Friday 9. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0889/0,0,932,1592/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-09 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Aunt Laura Roosevelt gave my
pretty darling a great
family dinner.
```

### 429_0889_R · April, Saturday 10. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0889/932,0,958,1592/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-10 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Dinner at home; I sat be-
-tween Miss Moran and Miss
Prince[?].
```

*1880-04-11 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Every body is just too
sweet for anything;
the cousins, and, even
more, dear old Nell are
just as attentive as they
can be, sending flowers &c.
Mother & the girls are
just splendid. All my
friends are behaving
beautifully.
```

```
Afternoon Tea; in evening
took Alice & Bamie to the
Harvard theatricals.

```

### 429_0892_L · April, Thursday 15. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0892/0,0,962,1594/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-15 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Aunt Annie gave a party
for Alice.
In the morning I drove
her way out beyond
Jerome Park
```

### 429_0892_R · April, Friday 16. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0892/962,0,990,1594/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-16 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Last day of our stay;
everyone has done all
things possible for Alice.
```

### 429_0893_L · April, Saturday 17. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0893/0,0,967,1594/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-17 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Mrs. Lee, Alice and I took 11 am
train for Boston. Went out
to Porc. supper to see Charley
Dickey initiated.
```

### 429_0893_R · April, Sunday 18. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0893/967,0,978,1594/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-18 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
After the Porc. Breakfast I drove
Dick over in the cart to
Chestnut Hill. I took Alice
out for a long drive in the
afternoon; it was perfectly
delicious, the weather being
beautiful.
The past three months have
been almost a dream of
happiness; no man could lead
a more ideal life than I do.
I have very little work to do,
only enough to employ me a
few hours a day during three or
four days in the week, and I can
cut whenever I please; I
spend several nights (usually
5) a week at Chestnut Hill,
where the whole family are just
too sweet to me for anything;
the other two evenings I spend in
the club; I drive almost every day
in my cart; and no man ever
loved and was beloved by so sweet,
tender and pretty a girl.
```
> 'could' underlined in original

### 429_0894_L · April, Monday 19. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0894/0,0,944,1571/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-19 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove Dick back to Cambridge.
```

### 429_0895_L · April, Wednesday 21. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0895/0,0,954,1612/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-21 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Poor old Mrs. Haskell, Alices
grandmother, is dead, and so
I went down to New Bedford
to the funeral.
```

### 429_0895_R · April, Thursday 22. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0895/954,0,983,1612/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-22 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove over in the afternoon
to Chestnut Hill, with
my bag under the seat,
as I intend to stay
several days. I took Alice
out driving.
```
> first letter of 'Drove' appears written over another letter

### 429_0896_L · April, Friday 23. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0896/0,0,969,1604/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-23 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
I drove back to Cambridge
in the morning, returning
to Chestnut Hill in the
afternoon. In the evening
we play whist, read, or the
girls play on the piano; and
I generally have an hour or
two by ~~hin~~[?] myself with my
own sweet little queen.
```
> a short word before 'myself' appears cancelled/overwritten; reading uncertain

### 429_0896_R · April, Saturday 24. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0896/969,0,954,1604/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-24 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Spent the morning with
Mamie S. and playing
with cunning little Bella.
In the afternoon I took
my bright, sunny darling
out for a long drive,
through Milton, Forest Hills
and Longwood.
```

### 429_0897_L · April, Sunday 25. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0897/0,0,966,1607/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-25 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to the dear little Chestnut
Hill church in the morning.
Everything out here is
perfectly ideal.
I took my darling a long
drive in the afternoon, through
way beyond the Charles
Words can not tell how I
love her; more and more
every day.
I doubt if any man
was ever more happy than
I am; I always enjoyed
myself, but I have never
been so happy as during these
last three months.
```

### 429_0897_R · April, Monday 26. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0897/966,0,976,1607/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-26 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the morning Alice studied and
practised music a couple of hours,
while I pumped the water up and studied;
then we played tennis; then she sewed
while I read Prescotts Conquest of
Peru aloud. In the afternoon I
took my sweet, sunny faced
darling for a three hours ride
in my dog cart, all round the country,
way up to Dedham and the Blue
Hills. After tea I read aloud
to Bella and Georgie, romped with
them, and told them stories - the
other girls all coming in as
auditors. Played whist till
nine oclock; then spent an hour
alone with my sweet queen.
This is a fair sample of my
days; and no wonder I am
supremely happy.
```

### 429_0898_L · April, Tuesday 27. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0898/0,0,979,1615/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-27 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge in the
morning.
```

### 429_0898_R · April, Wednesday 28. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0898/979,0,984,1615/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-28 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the afternoon played
tennis with Harry Chapin.
In the evening went to the
Pudding theatricals, which
were excellent.
I did not study much
the early part of this year,
and since my engagement
I have hardly studied atall.
I never work atall except
on three days (usually Tuesday,
Wednesday and either Thursday
or Friday), and do not
average more than five hours
on those days; but my marks
were so good the first three
years that I can afford to be
idle now.
```

### 429_0899_R · April, Friday 30. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0899/970,0,1000,1581/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-04-30 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove over in the afternoon
to Chestnut Hill and took
pretty Baby out driving.
```

### 429_0900_L · May, Saturday 1. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0900/0,0,963,1586/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-01 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Spent the morning romping
with Georgie and Bella &[?] playing
tennis with Rose S; in the
afternoon I took darling
out for a long drive; it
is the most beautiful
weather imaginable for
driving and tennis.
```
> conjunction before 'playing' cramped at line end

### 429_0900_R · May, Sunday 2. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0900/963,0,1003,1586/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-02 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went to the dear little
church in the morning; took
Alice out driving in the
afternoon. Chestnut Hill
is an absolutely ideal place;
and the Lee's house is
awfully pleasant to stay at;
they are all too sweet to me
for anything.
```
> 'stay at' written nearly joined

### 429_0901_L · May, Monday 3. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0901/0,0,962,1634/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-03 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the morning played
tennis with Rose & Rosy; in
the afternoon with Alice; and
then drove her in to Boston
and back. We spent the evening
at the Saltonstalls.
```

### 429_0901_R · May, Tuesday 4. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0901/962,0,1023,1634/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-04 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge in
the morning.
```

### 429_0902_L · May, Wednesday 5. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0902/0,0,942,1634/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-05 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
I drove Minot Weld up in
the cart to Frank Codman's
farm, at Lincoln, 12 miles
away. We spent all day
there, and had great fun.
I have certainly lived
like a prince for my last
two years in college. I have
had just as much money
as I could spend; belonged to
the Porcellian Club; have had
some capital hunting trips;
my life has been varied; I
have kept a good horse and
cart; I have had half a dozen
good and true friends in college,
and several very pleasant fam-
ilies outside; a lovely home;
I have had but little work, only
enough to give me an occupation;
and to crown all - infinitely above
everything else put together - I have
won the sweetest of girls for my wife.
No man ever had so pleasant a
college course.
```
> last line written below the final ruled line

### 429_0903_L · May, Friday 7. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0903/0,0,955,1623/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-07 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
I drove over to Chestnut
Hill in the afternoon. The
Miss Rotches are staying
at the Lees.
```

### 429_0903_R · May, Saturday 8. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0903/955,0,994,1623/full/0/default.jpg

```
The Miss Rotches ~~are~~ ^were^ at the Lees;
Miss Reynolds at the Saltonstalls;
Jack Tebbets[?] & Frank
Codman ^& Tom Lee^ drove over; and we
played tennis all day.
In the afternoon I drove
Jack back to a Porcel-
-lian dinner, given by
Harry Shaw, Dick and
myself especially for some
of the older honoraries,
```

```
After the Porc. breakfast
I drove Dick over to
Chestnut Hill; in the

in the cart.
```

Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0904/967,0,971,1623/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-10 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
The weather was so hot
that I took Alice out
in the cart after
dinner; we had a
delightful drive.
```

### 429_0905_L · May, Tuesday 11. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0905/0,0,1008,1621/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-11 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Early in the morning
I drove back to
Cambridge.
```

### 429_0906_R · May, Friday 14. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0906/963,0,990,1645/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-14 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove over in the afternoon
to ~~Cambridge~~ ^Chestnut Hill^. Played tennis
after tea.
```
> 'Cambridge' struck through with 'Chestnut Hill' written above.

### 429_0907_L · May, Saturday 15. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0907/0,0,974,1633/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-15 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove my darling little
queen in to Boston to
the Union Boat Club to
see the class races in which
we were beaten. Drove her
out to Chestnut Hill
afterwards, and very
swell I felt, in my
stylish turnout, with my
bewitchingly pretty compan-
ion, driving through
Beacon street.
```

### 429_0907_R · May, Sunday 16. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0907/974,0,998,1633/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-16 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Church in the morning;
then I took Cotty, Georgie,
& Bella out walking in
the woods. In the after-
-noon drove Baby darling
way through Waverly
& Belmont; a lovely drive.
```
> 'Cotty' and 'Baby' readings slightly uncertain in letterforms but likely.

*1880-05-17 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove Dick back to

Drove Dick over to make a
call on Miss Georgie Heyward,
Joe Whitney's fiançée; had
an absurd time. Went to

### 429_0910_L · May, Friday 21. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0910/0,0,955,1624/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-21 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Walked over to and back
from Cambridge for my
recitations. In the evening,
after dinner, took my sweet
queen out for a long
drive in the beautiful
moonlight.
```
> 'evening' is compressed and could be read 'evenig'.

### 429_0910_R · May, Saturday 22. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0910/955,0,987,1624/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-22 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove Tom Lee over to
the Athletic games
which were very good;
and thence to a
garden party at
the Dixwells. It was
beautiful moonlight,
and I drove Alice
home.
```

### 429_0911_L · May, Sunday 23. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0911/0,0,959,1529/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-23 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Fanny Peabody & Cotty
are staying at the
Saltonstalls; after services
in the dear little church
we all went to walk
in the woods. In the
afternoon I took pretty
Sunshine a long drive
through the beautiful
country round Auburndale.
```

### 429_0911_R · May, Monday 24. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0911/959,0,1011,1529/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-24 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Spent the whole day with
my dearly loved darling.
In the morning I read aloud
while she sewed; though she
looked so bewitchingly pretty
that I had to stop all the
time to pet & caress her.
In the afternoon I took
her out in the cart, and
after making several calls
we wound up at the
Whitneys, where we took
tea and had a very
jolly time, driving home
in the moonlight about
ten oclock.
```

### 429_0912_L · May, Tuesday 25. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0912/0,0,973,1549/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-25 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge.
```

### 429_0913_R · May, Friday 28. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0913/988,0,1014,1573/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-28 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove over to Chestnut
Hill
```

### 429_0914_L · May, Saturday 29. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0914/0,0,988,1569/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-05-29 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Spent the morning with
my beloved darling, reading
Pendennis aloud, while she
sewed. In the afternoon I
took her the longest drive
we have gone yet, through
Dedham, Needham, and almost
to Wellesly and back through
Grantville.
```
> 'N' of Needham formed like a V; 'Wellesly' as written.

### 429_0914_R · May, Sunday 30. 1880.

*1880-05-30 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Raining, so after Church
spent the day in the
house, telling stories to
Bella and Georgie, or
with my darling.
```

### 429_0917_L · June, Friday 4. 1880.

```
Took darling Baby out
driving in the cart.
I don't think any
man ever could have
had such a darling,
pretty, little witch of a
sweetheart as I have.
```

### 429_0917_R · June, Saturday 5. 1880.

```
Spent the day with my
darling, reading aloud &c;

### 429_0918_L · June, Sunday 6. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0918/0,0,986,1569/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-06 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Raining; so after church I
walked alone; and when
it cleared up with my
queen.
```

### 429_0918_R · June, Monday 7. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0918/986,0,1028,1569/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-07 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Drove back to Cambridge
for my first examination.
For 10 days now I
shall stay in Cambridge,
and grind for my
final examinations. I
have a good deal of
work to do to make up
for the time I have
loafed; and so shall
not go over to Chestnut
Hill till the 16th, my
last examination. I
shall work like a
trojan for ten[?] days.
```
> 'do' (line 8) is underlined. Word before 'days' in last line is smudged/blotted; 'ten' is a tentative reading. Ending of 'like' also blotted.

### 429_0919_L · June, Thursday 10. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0919/0,0,1032,1560/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-10 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Went round to call at the
Lanes; very nice girls; Van is
a lucky man—and a good
fellow.
```
> 'Van' reading likely but initial letter slightly ambiguous.

### 429_0920_R · June, Sunday 13. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0920/995,0,1019,1558/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-13 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Breakfasted in the club;
dined at the Lanes.
Am still up to my
ears in work; quite a
novel sensation, I have
loafed so long. I have
been studying nine or
ten hours a day; my
amusements being an
hour's walk or lawn tennis
in the afternoon and an
hours whist, ^in the club^ in the evening
I have missed my darling
dreadfully.
```
> 'in the club' inserted above the line after 'whist,'.

### 429_0921_L · June, Wednesday 16. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0921/0,0,981,1549/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-16 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Passed my last exam-
ination. I have worked
well these last ten
days; and in fact I
had to. In the after-
noon I drove or[?] in my
cart to the Lees; it was
delightful to get back
to the cheerful house, and
to see kind, hospitable
Mr & Mrs Lee, the pretty
girls and the jolly
little children; and far
more than anything
to be once again with
my own, best beloved
little Queen; I have missed
her so; oh, how sweet
and pretty she was.
```
> 'or' after 'drove' is as it appears (possibly short for 'over').

### 429_0921_R · June, Thursday 17. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0921/981,0,971,1549/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-17 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Spent the morning
playing with Georgie
and Bella; in the
afternoon took my
darling to drive; then
played tennis till
dusk.
```

### 429_0922_L · June, Friday 18. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0922/0,0,997,1570/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-18 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the evening I took
sweet Alice for the crowning
drive of the season. We
started about half past
six and came back in
the clear moonlight about
half past nine. The weather
was just sufficiently cool,
and the country marvel-
lously beautiful; for we
drove through the wild,
wooded, hilly region to
the north of Wellesly.
```

### 429_0922_R · June, Saturday 19. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0922/997,0,1007,1570/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-19 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove back to Cambridge;
went to a Porc supper;
and went in town to
meet Elliott at the
10.45 train; took him up to
the club afterwards.
```

### 429_0923_L · June, Sunday 20. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0923/0,0,968,1548/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-20 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Nell and I breakfasted
in the club; then loafed
about it till 4 o'clock
when I drove him
over to the Lees (where
Mother and the sisters
are staying) returning
after tea. The families
get on beautifully
together; and they are
all just as lovely as
possible.
```
> Numeral '4' before o'clock slightly ambiguous in form.

### 429_0923_R · June, Monday 21. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0923/968,0,1017,1548/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-21 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Breakfasted in the club
with Nell; drove him
over to spend the day
at the Lees; we played
lawn tennis, walked, sang
and had a lovely time.
```

### 429_0924_L · June, Tuesday 22. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0924/0,0,1019,1554/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-22 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Drove Nell over to dine
at the Lees. Afterwards
drove Alice to a garden
party at the Winthrops,
and back again, in the
beautiful moonlight; then
drove Nell home.
```

### 429_0924_R · June, Wednesday 23. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0924/1019,0,1006,1554/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-23 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
A whole party of us (all
our family, the Lees, Rose &
Mamie S., & the Peabodys)
went down to lunch at
the Lees in Salem, and
then drove over to spend the
night at the Peabodys.
We had a very jolly
time, dancing and
playing charades and
hide and go seek.
```
> 'Mamie S.' reading slightly uncertain.

### 429_0925_L · June, Thursday 24. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0925/0,0,1139,1571/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-24 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
We came up to Boston
in the morning. Ike Iselin[?]
came on to spend a
few days with me in
the afternoon; I took him
and Nell up to the
ΔKE theatricals, which were
very good, ending up at
the Club.
```
> Surname after 'Ike' uncertain (looks like 'Iselin').

### 429_0925_R · June, Friday 25. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0925/1139,0,864,1571/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-25 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Class Day. Jack Kingsford[?]
is also staying with me.
At 9.30 our class marched into
chapel (all in dress suits & silk hats)
at 11.30 into the theatre. At two
our spread in the gymnasium
began; I had my hands full
superintending &c; then at
5.15 came the exercises
round the tree; then we
went to the tea at Morgans.
At 8.15 I took possession of my
darling, and had my reward.
I had secured the same room
in Hollis that I had had last
year, and there we sat for
nearly two hours overlooking
the beautifully illuminated yard,
which looked like fairy land.
Alice was in the same dress as last
classday, and oh how marvellously pretty!
We then went to supper at the Lanes;
and thence I went to the club,
getting to bed at 4 o'clock.
```
> Transcribed from full frame full/429_0925.jpg because the half-image cuts off line beginnings. Surname after 'Jack' uncertain (initial H/K/T). 'and' in 'and had my reward' is blotted.

### 429_0926_L · JUNE, SATURDAY 26. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0926/0,0,1017,1540/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-261880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
We all breakfasted in the
club; then I took Kingsford
Iselin and Nell[?] over to the Lees,
in my cart, to dinner. After-
wards we went to a far-[?]
garden party at the Lowells.
```
> first word preceded by a stray mark; 'far-' uncertain; 'Nell' could be 'Bell'

### 429_0926_R · JUNE, SUNDAY 27. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0926/1017,0,995,1540/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-271880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Breakfasted in the Club
as usual; then drove over
to the Hill, in a round-
about way to show the
boys the country. In the
afternoon Iselin and
Kingsford returned to
N. Y. In the evening
I took my darling
out in the woods, and
we had a lovely stroll.
She grows sweeter and
prettier every day - and
yet she couldn't be
sweeter or prettier than
she is.
```
> 'couldn't' underlined in original

### 429_0927_L · JUNE, MONDAY 28. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0927/0,0,1030,1563/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-281880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
After a late breakfast I drove
Nell[?] over to the Lees, where we
spent the day. In the evening
my darling queen and I
went out walking in the
beautiful cool summer air.
```
> 'Nell' could be 'Bell'

### 429_0927_R · JUNE, TUESDAY 29. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0927/1030,0,1001,1563/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-291880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
All the rest went down to Nahant.
I stayed to pack up and pay
bills. To think of the four
years being over! I suppose under
other circumstances I should
feel melancholy; but with
Alice to love me life will
always seem laughing and
loving; who could feel blue
with so pure and lovely
and fond and beautiful
a little bride elect? Only
four months before we
get married! ~~[illegible]~~ My cup of
happiness is almost too full.
```
> a short false start before 'My' appears struck/abandoned

### 429_0928_L · JUNE, WEDNESDAY 30. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0928/0,0,999,1511/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-06-301880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Commencement Day. Received
my degree; I actually am
an alumnus! I can not
imagine any man's having
a more happy and satisfactory
four years than I have had.
Spent most of the day in the
club; drove over to a garden
party at the Dexters in the
evening; from there drove my
bewitching Queen to Chestnut
Hill; then drove Nell[?] back to
Cambridge.
```
> 'Nell' could be 'Bell'

### 429_0928_R · JULY, THURSDAY 1. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0928/999,0,965,1511/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-11880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Alice, Rosy and Rose
went back to Oyster
Bay with us; we
reached it late in
the evening. My cart
arrived safely with
the horse.
Looking back at
my college career it
is very pleasant to
feel that there is
nothing I should
care to change in
it; my career (both
in and out of college)
has been more suc-
cessful than that
of any man I
have known.
```
> 'my career' and 'has been more successful than that of any man I have known' underlined in original

### 429_0929_L · JULY, FRIDAY 2. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0929/0,0,1021,1550/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-21880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
It is perfectly delightful
to have my sweet
Queen here. In the
morning I drove her
with Rose and Ike[?] Iselin
on the back seat over
to Laurelton and Syosset.
In the afternoon we
played tennis. The
evening I spent with
her in the summer
house.
```
> 'Ike' uncertain

### 429_0929_R · JULY, SATURDAY 3. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0929/1021,0,976,1550/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-31880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the morning I
drove Alice through the
beautiful country round
about here. In the
afternoon we all
went out sailing.
In the evening we
went over to Uncle
Jim's. The cousins
and everyone are
just too sweet for
anything. Douglas
Robinson and Romy
Colgate are also staying
with us, so there is
quite a large party
in the house.
```

### 429_0930_L · JULY, SUNDAY 4. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0930/0,0,994,1558/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-41880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Church in the morning.
In the afternoon I took
my darling a long
and beautiful walk through
Fleet's woods; in the
evening we went down
to the dock.
How I love her! and
I would trust her to the
end of the world. Whatever
troubles come upon me -
losses or griefs or sickness -
I know she will only be
more true and loving and
tender than ever; she is so
radiantly pure and good and
beautiful that I almost
feel like worshipping her. Not
one thing is ever hidden between
us. No matter how long I live
I know my love for her will
only grow deeper and tenderer
day by day; and she shall al-
ways be mistress over all
that I have.
```

### 429_0930_R · JULY, MONDAY 5. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0930/994,0,1005,1558/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-51880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the morning I drove
darling baby over to
Cold Spring to get pond
lilies. In the evening
went to see some clever
acting at the Weekes.
```

### 429_0931_L · JULY, TUESDAY 6. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0931/0,0,1005,1580/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-61880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Took my Queen out
driving in the morning;
in the evening went to
tea at Aunt Annie's.
How intensely happy
I am! Most of the
day is spent with my
love.
```
> small blot/correction before 'spent'

### 429_0931_R · JULY, WEDNESDAY 7. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0931/1005,0,1010,1580/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-71880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
In the morning I
rowed Alice over to
Yellow Banks coming
back for lunch. We
played tennis in the
afternoon, and went
to tea at the Beek-
man's, where we had
a very jolly time.
```
> 'Beek-man's' read with some uncertainty; heavy show-through

### 429_0932_L · JULY, THURSDAY 8. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0932/0,0,1021,1573/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-81880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
In the morning I
rowed; in the afternoon
I took my darling
a long drive, through
"Old Kentuck" and
back of Glen Cove and[?]
Norwich; I spent the
evening in the summer
house with her.
```
> ink heavy/blurred; word after 'Glen Cove' smudged

### 429_0932_R · JULY, FRIDAY 9. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0932/1021,0,992,1573/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-91880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
In the morning I
went out sailing.
In the afternoon I
took my queen a
long drive over the
plains, and spent
the evening with her
in the summer house.
```

### 429_0933_L · JULY, SATURDAY 10. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0933/0,0,1025,1596/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-101880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
The three girls went back
to Chestnut Hill; I went
in town to see them off.
How I hate to part
with my bewitching
little queen, even for a
week! Oh, my own
heart's darling, how I
love and reverence you!
```

### 429_0934_L · JULY, WEDNESDAY 14. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0934/0,0,1017,1529/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-141880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · high*
```
Am leading the
usual Oyster Bay
life; tennis, rowing,
swimming and
sailing - but I
miss Alice too
much to care so
much as I used to
for things.
```

### 429_0934_R · JULY, THURSDAY 15. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0934/1017,0,937,1529/full/0/default.jpg

### 429_0935_R · JULY, SATURDAY 17. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0935/993,0,959,1531/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-171880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Went over to Chestnut
Hill; how lovely it
is to see my
darling once more!
Oh, how pretty she
looked and how
lovingly she greeted
me!
```

### 429_0936_L · JULY, SUNDAY 18. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0936/0,0,1005,1564/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-181880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Spent the entire day
with my darling.
Oh, how blessed I
am in her love; and
how tenderly I shall
love and cherish her.
She is the sweetest,
prettiest, purest, gentlest,
and noblest of women.
```
> first two lines heavily inked/blurred

### 429_0936_R · JULY, MONDAY 19. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0936/1005,0,953,1564/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-191880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
As yesterday, I spent
most of the day with
my Queen; for about
three hours I took
Georgie, Cottie[?] and
Bella on a
blackberry picnic.
```
> 'Cottie' uncertain; a stray mark after 'and'

### 429_0937_L · JULY, TUESDAY 20. 1880.
Scan: https://tile.loc.gov/image-services/iiif/service:mss:mss38299:mss38299_429:0937/0,0,1017,1567/full/0/default.jpg

*1880-07-201880 · entry · body · ink · hand TR · medium*
```
Rose and Richard, and
Alice and myself took
the night train for
Mt Desert; at 11 p.m.

```

### 429_0937_R · JULY, WEDNESDAY 21. 1880.

and playing

over to Otter Creek.
